METRIC    : area_delay_product

[SYSTEM]
You are an RTL (Register Transfer Level) design agent. Your task is to create a Verilog/SystemVerilog design that satisfies the given specification, is functionally correct, and has minimal cost (area_delay_product).

**Cost metric `area_delay_product`:** area × achieved critical-path delay — the classic area-delay product. Does NOT include simulation cycle count (contrast with the 'area_runtime_product' metric). Cost is measured on your design alone. The testbench and everything it owns (checking logic, the external memories, etc.) is not synthesized and contributes nothing to area, delay, power, or energy.

## Specification
Full 8x8 2-D IDCT of a baseline JPEG decoder (ultraembedded/core_jpeg), module
name `jpeg_idct_2d`: the row pass (jpeg_idct_x, OUT_SHIFT=11), a double-buffered
corner-turn (jpeg_idct_transpose with four 32x32 dual-port RAMs), and the column
pass (jpeg_idct_y, OUT_SHIFT=15), composed exactly as in the original decoder
(downstream ready tied high; the row pass accepts no backpressure). Each 1-D
pass is the factorized (AAN-style) algorithm with cosine constants scaled by
4096 (C1..C7 = 4017, 3784, 3406, 2896, 2276, 1567, 799), time-sharing 5
multipliers over an 8-beat schedule. ALL arithmetic is evaluated in 32-bit
signed context with wrap-around; outputs of each pass are arithmetic-shifted
right (11 then 15); the 1/sqrt(2) odd-part scaling is `(x * 181) / 256` where
`*` wraps at 32 bits and `/` is SIGNED division truncating toward zero. This
exact fixed-point behavior is what the golden vectors encode.

Interface (clocked, sync active-high reset `rst`; no backpressure):
  inputs : clk, rst, img_start_i (1-cycle pulse resets the output streamers),
           img_end_i (unused), inport_valid_i, inport_idx_i[2:0],
           inport_data0..3_i[15:0]
  outputs: outport_valid_o, outport_data_o[31:0], outport_idx_o[5:0]

Input protocol (the upstream block serializer's FIXED schedule, reproduced
exactly by the testbench): per row of 8 coefficients c0..c7 (16-bit signed),
8 back-to-back valid beats with idx = 0..7; the four data lanes carry the EVEN
set (c0,c2,c4,c6) on beats idx in {0,2,5,7} and the ODD set (c1,c3,c5,c7) on
beats idx in {1,3,4,6}. 8 rows back-to-back form a 64-beat block; between
blocks there are >= 2 idle cycles (valid=0, idx=0, data=0). Pipeline registers
advance every clock regardless of valid — designs must be robust to the gap
pattern exactly as the original is.

Output (fixed schedule): 64 values per block streamed on outport_valid_o; the
k-th streamed value of a block carries outport_idx_o = {k[2:0], k[5:3]} (the
column pass emits column-major; the idx label gives the ROW-MAJOR pixel
position) and must match the expected output at that position. The stream of a
block overlaps the next block's input beats (the transpose double-buffers one
full block).

A working starting point is provided: `starting_point.v` contains the
ORIGINAL core_jpeg modules (Apache 2.0), unmodified, under a thin composition
top. It passes all golden vectors (they were generated from it — the original
RTL is the correctness oracle). Study it, then optimize while preserving its
exact behavior. Sibling benchmark: `jpeg_idct_2d_spire` (identical tb.sv +
vectors.dat, bit-exact SpireHDL starting point). The 1-D row pass alone is
also available as the `jpeg_idct_x_*` benchmarks.

NOTE on memories: the cost-evaluation synthesis flow targets a standard-cell
library with no RAM macros, so every behavioral memory in this design (the
transpose buffers, block_out, etc.) is inferred as a register array plus mux
decode REGARDLESS of coding style. Optimizing that decode/mux logic is fair
game; instantiating a hard RAM macro is not possible in this flow.

## Tools
- create_file: Create a new file in your working directory
- replace_file: Replace the entire contents of an existing file
- apply_diff: Apply a unified diff to modify a file
- edit_file: Edit a file by replacing an exact string match with new content. Provide old_string (must match exactly once) and new_string.
- ls: List files in your working directory
- read_file: Read the contents of a file
- run_evaluation(filename[, target_delay]): Run evaluation on the given design file. Returns correctness (Verilator simulation) + cost (Yosys area_delay_product). The filename must be your main design file (e.g. 'design.sv'). Optionally pass target_delay (ps) to override the synthesis timing constraint.

## Strategy
1. Lay out an action plan. Try to cover a diverse set of approaches in your plan to increase the chances of finding a good solution within the step limit. Also once you find a new best solution, explore close solutions. Trade off exploration with exploitation.
2. First, create a simple, straightforward design that is functionally correct. Focus on getting 100% correctness first.
3. Run evaluation to confirm correctness and get the initial cost (area_delay_product).
4. ONLY after achieving 100% correctness, try to optimize the design to reduce the area_delay_product.
5. After each optimization, run evaluation to verify correctness is maintained and check the new cost.
6. If an optimization breaks correctness, revert and try a different approach.
7. Keep iterating until you run out of steps — use every step to try improvements. The run ends automatically at the step limit; your best passing design is kept.

In general, be creative and take risk. At least after you have a correct design, try bold optimizations to reduce area_delay_product, even if they might break correctness. You can always revert if it doesn't work.
The best results you obtain which is 100% correctness counts.
Try to beat at least the straightforward implementation.

Running evaluation is crucial after every change to ensure correctness and track cost. Do not skip evaluations.

You may do some 'thinking' alongside with your tool calling responses to explain your reasoning, but always remember to call a tool in every response.

## Important
- Always call a tool in every response.
- You have a maximum of **60 steps** (LLM turns). Budget them wisely: get a correct design first, then optimise. You may batch tool calls in one response --- at most 8, and only one `run_evaluation`.
- The best result is the one with lowest cost among designs that pass 100% of testbench checks.
- Use different filenames for different design variants so you can retrieve them later.
- If context files (e.g. component source files) are in your workspace, you can modify them directly using edit_file or replace_file to optimize the design. Make targeted, incremental changes rather than rewriting files from scratch — small edits are less likely to introduce bugs.
- Write clean, synthesizable RTL code.
- You can pass `target_delay` (ps) to `run_evaluation` to try different synthesis timing constraints; experimenting with this may improve PPA results (lower target delay may reduce delay, higher target delay may reduce area/transistor count). target_delay must be > 0.
- Do not spend too many steps sweeping `target_delay` — basically if you want to minimize delay choose a low value,if you want to minimize area/transistor count choose a high value. That's it.A few targeted experiments are more productive than an exhaustive sweep.

## Delay Optimization Tips
- `target_delay` trades area against delay (a lower value pressures toward faster logic, a higher value lets Yosys re-map to smaller cells). Do not overly iterate on different target delays; often the RTL structure is the real lever, target_delay is only a minor secondary knob.
- After getting a correct design, try different accumulation orderings (sequential vs. tree) and compare results — Yosys synthesis can produce noticeably different delays (~10 ps) from structurally similar descriptions.
- For designs dominated by a multiply-accumulate chain, the critical path is the multiplier followed by the adder tree. Reducing the adder depth or splitting into parallel partial sums can help.

## Reference Implementations
The following are reference implementations for inspiration. Study them for patterns and techniques, but do not copy them directly — adapt ideas to your specific design.

### Parallel-prefix adder (ripple-carry / carry-lookahead) (Add.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Binary adder using ripple-carry or parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.
// S = A+B

module Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);

	// Function: Binary adder using parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.

	logic [width-1:0] GI, PI;  // prefix gen./prop. in
	logic [width-1:0] GO, PO;  // prefix gen./prop. out
	logic [width-1:0] PT;  // adder propagate temp

	// Internal signals for unsigned operands
	logic [width-1:0] Auns, Buns, Suns;

	// default ripple-carry adder as slow implementation
	if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin
		// type conversion: std_logic_vector -> unsigned
		assign Auns = A;
		assign Buns = B;

		// addition
		assign Suns = Auns + Buns;

		// type conversion: unsigned -> std_logic_vector
		assign S = Suns;
	end else begin
		// parallel-prefix adders as medium and fast implementations

		// calculate prefix input generate/propagate signals
		assign GI = A & B;
		assign PI = A | B;
		// calculate adder propagate signals (PT = A xor B)
		assign PT = ~GI & PI;

		// calculate prefix output generate/propagate signals
		PrefixAndOr #(
			.width(width),
			.speed(speed)
		) prefix (
			.GI(GI),
			.PI(PI),
			.GO(GO),
			.PO(PO)
		);

		// calculate sum bits
		assign S = PT ^ {GO[width-2:0], 1'b0};
	end
endmodule



module behavioural_Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);
	assign S = A + B;
endmodule
```

### Prefix AND-OR structures (Sklansky, Brent-Kung, serial) (PrefixAndOr.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Prefix structures of different depth (i.e. speed) for carry calculation
// in binary adders. Compute in m levels new generate/propagate signal pairs
// for always larger groups of bits. Generate signals of the last level correspond
// to carries in binary addition. Basic logic operations: AND-OR for generate
// signals, AND for propagate signals.

module PrefixAndOr #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] GI,  // gen./prop. in
	input  logic [width-1:0] PI,  // gen./prop. in
	output logic [width-1:0] GO,  // gen./prop. out
	output logic [width-1:0] PO   // gen./prop. out
);

	// Constants
	localparam int n = width;  // prefix structure width
	localparam int m = $clog2(width);  // prefix structure depth

	// Sklansky parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::FAST) begin : fastPrefix
		logic [(m+1)*n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
			assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
			assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;
			for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels
				for (genvar k = 0; k < 2 ** (m - l); k++) begin : groups
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2 ** (l - 1); i++) begin : bits
						// pass prop and gen to following nodes
						if ((k * 2 ** l + i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = GT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
							assign PT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = PT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
						end
						// calculate new propagate and generate
						if ((k * 2 ** l + 2 ** (l - 1) + i) < n) begin : black
							assign GT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
												GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
											  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												 & GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1] );
							assign PT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
													PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1];
						end
					end
				end
			end
			assign GO = GT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
			assign PO = PT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
	end

	// Brent-Kung parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::MEDIUM) begin : mediumPrefix
		logic [(2*m)*n -1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
		assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;

		for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels1
			for (genvar k = 0; k < 2**(m-l); k++) begin : groups
				for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**l -1; i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**l +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
					end // white
				end // bits
				if ((k* 2**l + 2**l -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l - 1] |
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							     & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**(l-1) -1]);
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) -1];
				end // black
			end
		end // level1
		for (genvar l = m +1; l < 2*m; l++) begin : levels2
			for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
				if (i < n) begin : white
					assign GT[l*n +i] = GT[(l-1)*n +i];
					assign PT[l*n +i] = PT[(l-1)*n +i];
				end // white
			end // bits
			for (genvar k = 1; k < 2**(l-m); k++) begin : groups
				if (l < 2*m -1) begin : empty
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l -1) -1; i++) begin : bits
						if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
							assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						end // white
					end
				end // empty
				if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l -1) -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m-l-1) -1] 
								 & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) -1] );
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) -1];
				end // black
				for (genvar i = 2**(2*m -l-1); i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
					end // white
				end
			end
		end // level2
		assign GO = GT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
		assign PO = PT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
	end  // Serial-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	else if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin : slowPrefix
		logic [n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[0] = GI[0];
		assign PT[0] = PI[0];
		
		for (genvar i = 1; i < n; i++) begin : bits
			assign GT[i] = GI[i] | (PI[i] & GT[i-1]);
			assign PT[i] = PI[i] & PT[i-1];
		end
		assign GO = GT;
		assign PO = PT;
	end

endmodule

```

// jpeg_idct_2d — full 8x8 2-D IDCT of the baseline JPEG decoder
// (ultraembedded/core_jpeg, Apache 2.0): the verbatim composition of

wire signed [31:0] block_in_0_1 = {{16{inport_data0_i[15]}}, inport_data0_i};
wire signed [31:0] block_in_2_3 = {{16{inport_data1_i[15]}}, inport_data1_i};
wire signed [31:0] block_in_4_5 = {{16{inport_data2_i[15]}}, inport_data2_i};
wire signed [31:0] block_in_6_7 = {{16{inport_data3_i[15]}}, inport_data3_i};

//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// IDCT
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
reg signed [31:0] i0;
reg signed [31:0] mul0_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul0_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul1_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul1_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul2_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul2_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul3_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul3_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul4_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul4_b;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    i0     <= 32'b0;
    mul0_a <= 32'b0;
    mul0_b <= 32'b0;
    mul1_a <= 32'b0;
    mul1_b <= 32'b0;
    mul2_a <= 32'b0;
    mul2_b <= 32'b0;
    mul3_a <= 32'b0;
    mul3_b <= 32'b0;
    mul4_a <= 32'b0;
    mul4_b <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    /* verilator lint_off WIDTH */
    case (inport_idx_i)
    3'd0:
    begin
        i0     <= block_in_0_1 + block_in_4_5;
        mul0_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul0_b <= C2_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C6_16;
    end
    3'd1:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1;
        mul0_b <= C1_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C7_16;
        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5;
        mul2_b <= C5_16;
        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul3_b <= C3_16;
        mul4_a <= i0;
        mul4_b <= C4_16;
    end
    3'd2:
    begin
        i0     <= block_in_0_1 - block_in_4_5;
    end
    3'd3:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1;
        mul0_b <= C7_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C1_16;
        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5;
        mul2_b <= C3_16;
        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul3_b <= C5_16;
    end
    3'd4:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1;
        mul0_b <= C7_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C1_16;
        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5;
        mul2_b <= C3_16;
        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul3_b <= C5_16;
    end
    3'd5:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul0_b <= C6_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C2_16;
        mul4_a <= i0;
        mul4_b <= C4_16;
    end
    default:
        ;
    endcase
    /* verilator lint_on WIDTH */
end

reg signed [31:0] mul0_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul1_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul2_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul3_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul4_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    mul0_q <= 32'b0;
    mul1_q <= 32'b0;
    mul2_q <= 32'b0;
    mul3_q <= 32'b0;
    mul4_q <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    mul0_q <= mul0_a * mul0_b;
    mul1_q <= mul1_a * mul1_b;
    mul2_q <= mul2_a * mul2_b;
    mul3_q <= mul3_a * mul3_b;
    mul4_q <= mul4_a * mul4_b;
end

reg signed [31:0] mul0;
reg signed [31:0] mul1;
reg signed [31:0] mul2;
reg signed [31:0] mul3;
reg signed [31:0] mul4;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    mul0 <= 32'b0;
    mul1 <= 32'b0;
    mul2 <= 32'b0;
    mul3 <= 32'b0;
    mul4 <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    mul0 <= mul0_q;
    mul1 <= mul1_q;
    mul2 <= mul2_q;
    mul3 <= mul3_q;
    mul4 <= mul4_q;
end

reg        out_stg0_valid_q;
reg [2:0]  out_stg0_idx_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg0_valid_q <= 1'b0;
    out_stg0_idx_q   <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg0_valid_q <= inport_valid_i;
    out_stg0_idx_q   <= inport_idx_i;
end

reg        out_stg1_valid_q;
reg [2:0]  out_stg1_idx_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg1_valid_q <= 1'b0;
    out_stg1_idx_q   <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg1_valid_q <= out_stg0_valid_q;
    out_stg1_idx_q   <= out_stg0_idx_q;
end

reg        out_stg2_valid_q;
reg [2:0]  out_stg2_idx_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg2_valid_q <= 1'b0;
    out_stg2_idx_q   <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg2_valid_q <= out_stg1_valid_q;
    out_stg2_idx_q   <= out_stg1_idx_q;
end

reg signed [31:0] o_s5;
reg signed [31:0] o_s6;
reg signed [31:0] o_s7;
reg signed [31:0] o_t0;
reg signed [31:0] o_t1;
reg signed [31:0] o_t2;
reg signed [31:0] o_t3;
reg signed [31:0] o_t4;
reg signed [31:0] o_t5;
reg signed [31:0] o_t6;
reg signed [31:0] o_t7;
reg signed [31:0] o_t6_5;
reg signed [31:0] o_t5_6;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    o_s5   <= 32'b0;
    o_s6   <= 32'b0;
    o_s7   <= 32'b0;
    o_t0   <= 32'b0;
    o_t1   <= 32'b0;
    o_t2   <= 32'b0;
    o_t3   <= 32'b0;
    o_t4   <= 32'b0;
    o_t5   <= 32'b0;
    o_t6   <= 32'b0;
    o_t7   <= 32'b0;
    o_t6_5 <= 32'b0;
    o_t5_6 <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    case (out_stg2_idx_q)
    3'd0:
    begin
        o_t3 <= mul0 + mul1; // s3
    end
    3'd1:
    begin
        o_s7 <= mul0 + mul1;
        o_s6 <= mul2 + mul3;
        o_t0 <= mul4;        // s0
    end
    3'd2:
    begin
        o_t0 <= o_t0 + o_t3; // t0
        o_t3 <= o_t0 - o_t3; // t3
        o_t7 <= o_s6 + o_s7;
    end
    3'd3:
    begin
        o_t4 <= (mul0 - mul1) + (mul2 - mul3);
    end
    3'd4:
    begin
        o_t0 <= mul0 - mul1; // s4
        o_s5 <= mul2 - mul3;    
    end
    3'd5:
    begin
        o_t3 <= mul0 - mul1; // s2
        o_t4 <= mul4; // s1
        o_t5 <= o_t0 - o_s5;
        o_t6 <= o_s7 - o_s6;
    end
    3'd6:
    begin
        o_t1 <= o_t4 + o_t3;
        o_t2 <= o_t4 - o_t3;
        o_t6_5 <= o_t6 - o_t5;
        o_t5_6 <= o_t5 + o_t6;
    end
    default:
    begin
        o_s5 <= (o_t6_5 * 181) / 256; // 1/sqrt(2)
        o_s6 <= (o_t5_6 * 181) / 256; // 1/sqrt(2)
    end
    endcase
end

reg        out_stg3_valid_q;
reg [2:0]  out_stg3_idx_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg3_valid_q <= 1'b0;
    out_stg3_idx_q   <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg3_valid_q <= out_stg2_valid_q;
    out_stg3_idx_q   <= out_stg2_idx_q;
end

reg signed [31:0] block_out[0:7];
reg signed [31:0] block_out_tmp;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    block_out[0] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[1] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[2] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[3] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[4] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[5] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[6] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[7] <= 32'b0;
    block_out_tmp <= 32'b0;
end
else if (out_stg3_valid_q)
begin
    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd3)
    begin
        block_out[0] <= ((o_t0 + o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out_tmp <= ((o_t0 - o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT); // block_out[7]
        block_out[3] <= ((o_t3 + o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[4] <= ((o_t3 - o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
    end

    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd6)
        block_out[7] <= block_out_tmp;

    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd7)
    begin
        block_out[2] <= ((o_t2 + o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[5] <= ((o_t2 - o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[1] <= ((o_t1 + o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[6] <= ((o_t1 - o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
    end
end

reg [7:0] valid_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    valid_q  <= 8'b0;
else if (img_start_i)
    valid_q  <= 8'b0;
else
    valid_q <= {valid_q[6:0], out_stg3_valid_q};

reg [5:0] ptr_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    ptr_q <= 6'd0;
else if (img_start_i)
    ptr_q <= 6'd0;
else if (outport_valid_o)
    ptr_q <= ptr_q + 6'd1;

assign outport_valid_o = valid_q[6];
assign outport_data_o  = block_out[ptr_q[2:0]];

assign outport_idx_o   = ptr_q;



endmodule

module jpeg_idct_transpose
(
    // Inputs
     input           clk
    ,input           rst
    ,input           img_start_i
    ,input           img_end_i
    ,input           inport_valid_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  inport_data_i
    ,input  [  5:0]  inport_idx_i
    ,input           outport_ready_i

    // Outputs
    ,output          inport_accept_o
    ,output          outport_valid_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data0_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data1_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data2_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data3_o
    ,output [  2:0]  outport_idx_o
);



reg         block_wr_q;
reg         block_rd_q;
reg [5:0]   rd_idx_q;
reg [3:0]   rd_addr_q;

wire [4:0]  wr_ptr_w = {block_wr_q, inport_idx_i[3:0]};

wire [31:0] outport_data0_w;
wire [31:0] outport_data1_w;
wire [31:0] outport_data2_w;
wire [31:0] outport_data3_w;

wire wr0_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd0);
wire wr1_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd1);
wire wr2_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd2);
wire wr3_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd3);

jpeg_idct_transpose_ram
u_ram0
(
     .clk0_i(clk)
    ,.rst0_i(rst)
    ,.clk1_i(clk)
    ,.rst1_i(rst)

    ,.addr0_i(wr_ptr_w)
    ,.data0_i(inport_data_i)
    ,.wr0_i(wr0_w)
    ,.data0_o()

    ,.addr1_i({block_rd_q, rd_addr_q})
    ,.data1_i(32'b0)
    ,.wr1_i(1'b0)
    ,.data1_o(outport_data0_w)
);

jpeg_idct_transpose_ram
u_ram1
(
     .clk0_i(clk)
    ,.rst0_i(rst)
    ,.clk1_i(clk)
    ,.rst1_i(rst)

    ,.addr0_i(wr_ptr_w)
    ,.data0_i(inport_data_i)
    ,.wr0_i(wr1_w)
    ,.data0_o()

    ,.addr1_i({block_rd_q, rd_addr_q})
    ,.data1_i(32'b0)
    ,.wr1_i(1'b0)
    ,.data1_o(outport_data1_w)
);

jpeg_idct_transpose_ram
u_ram2
(
     .clk0_i(clk)
    ,.rst0_i(rst)
    ,.clk1_i(clk)
    ,.rst1_i(rst)

    ,.addr0_i(wr_ptr_w)
    ,.data0_i(inport_data_i)
    ,.wr0_i(wr2_w)
    ,.data0_o()

    ,.addr1_i({block_rd_q, rd_addr_q})
    ,.data1_i(32'b0)
    ,.wr1_i(1'b0)
    ,.data1_o(outport_data2_w)
);

jpeg_idct_transpose_ram
u_ram3
(
     .clk0_i(clk)
    ,.rst0_i(rst)
    ,.clk1_i(clk)
    ,.rst1_i(rst)

    ,.addr0_i(wr_ptr_w)
    ,.data0_i(inport_data_i)
    ,.wr0_i(wr3_w)
    ,.data0_o()

    ,.addr1_i({block_rd_q, rd_addr_q})
    ,.data1_i(32'b0)
    ,.wr1_i(1'b0)
    ,.data1_o(outport_data3_w)
);

//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// Input Buffer
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
reg [1:0] block_ready_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    block_ready_q     <= 2'b0;
    block_wr_q        <= 1'b0;
    block_rd_q        <= 1'b0;
end
else if (img_start_i)
begin
    block_ready_q     <= 2'b0;
    block_wr_q        <= 1'b0;
    block_rd_q        <= 1'b0;
end
else
begin
    if (inport_valid_i && inport_idx_i == 6'd63 && inport_accept_o)
    begin
        block_ready_q[block_wr_q] <= 1'b1;
        block_wr_q                <= ~block_wr_q;
    end

    if (outport_valid_o && rd_idx_q[5:0] == 6'd63)
    begin
        block_ready_q[block_rd_q] <= 1'b0;
        block_rd_q <= ~block_rd_q;
    end
end

assign inport_accept_o = ~block_ready_q[block_wr_q];

//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// FSM
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
localparam STATE_W           = 2;
localparam STATE_IDLE        = 2'd0;
localparam STATE_SETUP       = 2'd1;
localparam STATE_ACTIVE      = 2'd2;

reg [STATE_W-1:0] state_q;
reg [STATE_W-1:0] next_state_r;

always @ *
begin
    next_state_r = state_q;

    case (state_q)
    STATE_IDLE:
    begin
        if (block_ready_q[block_rd_q] && outport_ready_i)
            next_state_r = STATE_SETUP;
    end
    STATE_SETUP:
    begin
        next_state_r = STATE_ACTIVE;
    end
    STATE_ACTIVE:
    begin
        if (outport_valid_o && rd_idx_q == 6'd63)
            next_state_r = STATE_IDLE;
    end
    default: ;
    endcase

    if (img_start_i)
        next_state_r = STATE_IDLE;
end

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    state_q <= STATE_IDLE;
else
    state_q <= next_state_r;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    rd_idx_q <= 6'b0;
else if (img_start_i)
    rd_idx_q <= 6'b0;
else if (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE)
    rd_idx_q <= rd_idx_q + 6'd1;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    rd_addr_q <= 4'b0;
else if (state_q == STATE_IDLE)
    rd_addr_q <= 4'b0;
else if (state_q == STATE_SETUP)
    rd_addr_q <= 4'd8;
else if (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE)
begin
    case (rd_idx_q[2:0])
    3'd0: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q - 4'd8;
    3'd1: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q + 4'd8;
    3'd2: ;
    3'd3: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q - 4'd8;
    3'd4: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q + 4'd8;
    3'd5: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q - 4'd8;
    3'd6: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q + 4'd1;
    3'd7: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q + 4'd8;
    endcase
end

assign outport_valid_o = (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE);
assign outport_idx_o   = rd_idx_q[2:0];
assign outport_data0_o = outport_data0_w;
assign outport_data1_o = outport_data1_w;
assign outport_data2_o = outport_data2_w;
assign outport_data3_o = outport_data3_w;


endmodule

module jpeg_idct_transpose_ram
(
    // Inputs
     input           clk0_i
    ,input           rst0_i
    ,input  [  4:0]  addr0_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  data0_i
    ,input           wr0_i
    ,input           clk1_i
    ,input           rst1_i
    ,input  [  4:0]  addr1_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  data1_i
    ,input           wr1_i

    // Outputs
    ,output [ 31:0]  data0_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  data1_o
);



//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// Dual Port RAM
// Mode: Read First
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
/* verilator lint_off MULTIDRIVEN */
reg [31:0]   ram [31:0] /*verilator public*/;
/* verilator lint_on MULTIDRIVEN */

reg [31:0] ram_read0_q;
reg [31:0] ram_read1_q;


// Synchronous write
always @ (posedge clk0_i)
begin
    if (wr0_i)
        ram[addr0_i][31:0] <= data0_i[31:0];

    ram_read0_q <= ram[addr0_i];
end

always @ (posedge clk1_i)
begin
    if (wr1_i)
        ram[addr1_i][31:0] <= data1_i[31:0];

    ram_read1_q <= ram[addr1_i];
end


assign data0_o = ram_read0_q;
assign data1_o = ram_read1_q;



endmodule

module jpeg_idct_y
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// Params
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
#(
     parameter OUT_SHIFT        = 15
    ,parameter INPUT_WIDTH      = 32
)
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// Ports
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
(
    // Inputs
     input           clk
    ,input           rst
    ,input           img_start_i
    ,input           img_end_i
    ,input           inport_valid_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  inport_data0_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  inport_data1_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  inport_data2_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  inport_data3_i
    ,input  [  2:0]  inport_idx_i

    // Outputs
    ,output          outport_valid_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data_o
    ,output [  5:0]  outport_idx_o
);




localparam [15:0] C1_16 = 4017; // cos( pi/16) x4096

        o_t5_6 <= o_t5 + o_t6;

        o_s5 <= (o_t6_5 * 181) / 256; // 1/sqrt(2)

// Self-checking testbench for jpeg_idct_2d — the full 8x8 2-D IDCT
// (x-pass -> transpose -> y-pass) of ultraembedded/core_jpeg.
// Reads vectors.dat: one line per 8x8 block = 64 input coefficients (16-bit
// signed, 4 hex digits, row-major c[row][col]) followed by 64 expected outputs
// (32-bit, 8 hex digits, in ROW-MAJOR pixel order — outputs are matched via
// outport_idx_o, which labels each streamed value with its row-major position).
//
// Drive protocol: identical to the jpeg_idct_x benchmark (the upstream block
// serializer's fixed schedule): per row 8 back-to-back valid beats idx=0..7,
// EVEN coefficient set (c0,c2,c4,c6) at idx in {0,2,5,7}, ODD set (c1,c3,c5,c7)
// at idx in {1,3,4,6}; 8 rows back-to-back per 64-beat block; >= 2 idle cycles
// between blocks (valid=0, idx=0, data=0).
//
// Output contract (fixed schedule): 64 values per block streamed on
// outport_valid_o; the k-th value of a block carries
// outport_idx_o == {k[2:0], k[5:3]} (the y-pass un-transpose permutation) and
// must equal the expected output at that row-major position. Comparison is
// EXACT (integer outputs).
module tb;
  int total_checks;
  int total_errors;

  logic        clk;
  logic        rst;
  logic        img_start;
  logic        inport_valid;
  logic [15:0] inport_data0, inport_data1, inport_data2, inport_data3;
  logic [2:0]  inport_idx;
  logic        outport_valid;
  logic [31:0] outport_data;
  logic [5:0]  outport_idx;

  jpeg_idct_2d dut (
    .clk(clk), .rst(rst),
    .img_start_i(img_start), .img_end_i(1'b0),
    .inport_valid_i(inport_valid),
    .inport_data0_i(inport_data0), .inport_data1_i(inport_data1),
    .inport_data2_i(inport_data2), .inport_data3_i(inport_data3),
    .inport_idx_i(inport_idx),
    .outport_valid_o(outport_valid),
    .outport_data_o(outport_data),
    .outport_idx_o(outport_idx)
  );

  initial clk = 1'b0;
  always #5 clk = ~clk;

  // ---- load vectors.dat: flat token stream, 128 tokens per block ----------
  localparam MAX_BLOCKS = 1024;
  logic [15:0] coeffs [0:MAX_BLOCKS*64-1];
  logic [31:0] expy   [0:MAX_BLOCKS*64-1];
  int num_blocks;

  task automatic load_vectors;
    int fd, rc, n;
    logic [31:0] tok;
    logic [31:0] flat [0:MAX_BLOCKS*128-1];
    fd = $fopen("vectors.dat", "r");
    if (fd == 0) begin
      $display("ERROR: cannot open vectors.dat");
      $fatal(1);
    end
    n = 0;
    while (!$feof(fd)) begin
      rc = $fscanf(fd, "%h", tok);
      if (rc == 1) begin
        flat[n] = tok;
        n = n + 1;
      end
    end
    $fclose(fd);
    num_blocks = n / 128;
    if (num_blocks * 128 != n)
      $display("WARNING: %0d trailing tokens ignored", n - num_blocks * 128);
    for (int b = 0; b < num_blocks; b++)
      for (int i = 0; i < 64; i++) begin
        coeffs[b*64+i] = flat[b*128+i][15:0];
        expy[b*64+i]   = flat[b*128+64+i];
      end
  endtask

  // ---- output checker -------------------------------------------------------
  int out_cnt;
  bit checking;
  logic [5:0] k;

  always @(posedge clk) begin
    #1;
    if (checking && outport_valid) begin
      total_checks = total_checks + 1;
      k = out_cnt % 64;
      if (outport_idx !== {k[2:0], k[5:3]}) begin
        $display("TB_ERROR block=%0d out=%0d idx=%0d expected_idx=%0d",
                 out_cnt / 64, out_cnt % 64, outport_idx, {k[2:0], k[5:3]});
        total_errors = total_errors + 1;
      end
      if (outport_data !== expy[(out_cnt / 64) * 64 + outport_idx]) begin
        $display("TB_ERROR block=%0d pos=%0d expected_y=%08x actual_y=%08x",
                 out_cnt / 64, outport_idx, expy[(out_cnt / 64) * 64 + outport_idx],
                 outport_data);

... (showing lines 1-100 of 169; use offset/limit for more)

  -> read_file(filename='tb.sv', offset=100)

[Step 8/60]
                 outport_data);
        total_errors = total_errors + 1;
      end
`ifdef DUMP_Y
      $display("Y %02x %08x", outport_idx, outport_data);
`endif
      out_cnt = out_cnt + 1;
    end
  end

  // ---- driver ----------------------------------------------------------------
  task automatic drive_beat(input logic valid, input logic [2:0] kk,
                            input logic [15:0] d0, d1, d2, d3);
    @(negedge clk);
    inport_valid = valid;
    inport_idx   = kk;
    inport_data0 = d0;
    inport_data1 = d1;
    inport_data2 = d2;
    inport_data3 = d3;
  endtask

  initial begin
    total_checks = 0;
    total_errors = 0;
    out_cnt = 0;
    checking = 0;
    rst = 1;
    img_start = 0;
    inport_valid = 0;
    inport_idx = 0;
    inport_data0 = 0; inport_data1 = 0; inport_data2 = 0; inport_data3 = 0;

    load_vectors();

    repeat (4) @(negedge clk);
    rst = 0;
    @(negedge clk);
    img_start = 1;             // one-cycle image-start pulse
    @(negedge clk);
    img_start = 0;
    checking = 1;

    for (int b = 0; b < num_blocks; b++) begin
      for (int r = 0; r < 8; r++) begin
        for (int kb = 0; kb < 8; kb++) begin
          if (kb == 0 || kb == 2 || kb == 5 || kb == 7)
            drive_beat(1'b1, kb[2:0], coeffs[b*64+r*8+0], coeffs[b*64+r*8+2],
                                      coeffs[b*64+r*8+4], coeffs[b*64+r*8+6]);
          else
            drive_beat(1'b1, kb[2:0], coeffs[b*64+r*8+1], coeffs[b*64+r*8+3],
                                      coeffs[b*64+r*8+5], coeffs[b*64+r*8+7]);
        end
      end
      // inter-block gap: >= 2 idle cycles (serializer IDLE+SETUP), varied a bit
      repeat (2 + (b % 3)) drive_beat(1'b0, 3'd0, 16'd0, 16'd0, 16'd0, 16'd0);
    end
    drive_beat(1'b0, 3'd0, 16'd0, 16'd0, 16'd0, 16'd0);
    repeat (400) @(negedge clk);  // flush x + transpose + y pipeline

    if (out_cnt != num_blocks * 64) begin
      $display("TB_ERROR missing outputs: got %0d, expected %0d", out_cnt, num_blocks * 64);
      total_errors = total_errors + 1;
    end
    $display("TB_SUMMARY total=%0d errors=%0d", total_checks, total_errors);
    if (total_errors != 0) $fatal(1, "FAIL");
    $display("PASS");
    $finish;
  end
endmodule

... (showing lines 100-169 of 169; use offset/limit for more)

[ASSISTANT]
Now I understand the full design. Let me create my first optimized version. Key observations:
1. The `ptr_conv` function in `jpeg_idct_y` is a 64-entry lookup table that's just a bit swap `{ptr_q[2:0], ptr_q[5:3]}` - huge area waste
2. The transpose RAMs have unnecessary read-on-write-port logic
3. The critical path is through the transpose RAM address decode

Let me create an optimized version starting with the easy wins:

reg signed [31:0] block_out[0:7];
reg signed [31:0] block_out_tmp;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst) begin
    block_out[0] <= 32'b0; block_out[1] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[2] <= 32'b0; block_out[3] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[4] <= 32'b0; block_out[5] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[6] <= 32'b0; block_out[7] <= 32'b0;
    block_out_tmp <= 32'b0;
end else if (out_stg3_valid_q) begin
    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd3) begin
        block_out[0] <= ((o_t0 + o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out_tmp <= ((o_t0 - o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[3] <= ((o_t3 + o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[4] <= ((o_t3 - o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
    end
    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd6)
        block_out[7] <= block_out_tmp;
    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd7) begin
        block_out[2] <= ((o_t2 + o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[5] <= ((o_t2 - o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[1] <= ((o_t1 + o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[6] <= ((o_t1 - o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
    end
end

reg [7:0] valid_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    valid_q  <= 8'b0;
else if (img_start_i)
    valid_q  <= 8'b0;
else
    valid_q <= {valid_q[6:0], out_stg3_valid_q};

reg [5:0] ptr_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    ptr_q <= 6'd0;
else if (img_start_i)
    ptr_q <= 6'd0;
else if (outport_valid_o)
    ptr_q <= ptr_q + 6'd1;

assign outport_valid_o = valid_q[6];
assign outport_data_o  = block_out[ptr_q[2:0]];
assign outport_idx_o   = ptr_q;

endmodule

//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// jpeg_idct_transpose: double-buffered corner-turn
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
module jpeg_idct_transpose
(
    input           clk
    ,input           rst
    ,input           img_start_i
    ,input           img_end_i
    ,input           inport_valid_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  inport_data_i
    ,input  [  5:0]  inport_idx_i
    ,input           outport_ready_i
    ,output          inport_accept_o
    ,output          outport_valid_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data0_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data1_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data2_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data3_o
    ,output [  2:0]  outport_idx_o
);

reg         block_wr_q;
reg         block_rd_q;
reg [5:0]   rd_idx_q;
reg [3:0]   rd_addr_q;

wire [4:0]  wr_ptr_w = {block_wr_q, inport_idx_i[3:0]};

[ASSISTANT]
Good, v1 passes at 9.25M. The critical path is the 32:1 mux in the transpose RAM. Let me try two optimizations: (1) remove the unnecessary `mul_q → mul` pipeline stage, and (2) split the 32-entry RAMs into 16-entry halves with explicit 2:1 output mux.

 [15:0]   inport_data2_i\n    ,input  [15:0]   inport_data3_i\n    ,input  [2:0]    inport_idx_i\n    ,output          outport_valid_o\n    ,output [31:0]   outport_data_o\n    ,output [5:0]    outport_idx_o\n);\n\nlocalparam [15:0] C1_16 = 4017, C2_16 = 3784, C3_16 = 3406, C4_16 = 2896;\nlocalparam [15:0] C5_16 = 2276, C6_16 = 1567, C7_16 = 799;\n\nwire signed [31:0] block_in_0_1 = {{16{inport_data0_i[15]}}, inport_data0_i};\nwire signed [31:0] block_in_2_3 = {{16{inport_data1_i[15]}}, inport_data1_i};\nwire signed [31:0] block_in_4_5 = {{16{inport_data2_i[15]}}, inport_data2_i};\nwire signed [31:0] block_in_6_7 = {{16{inport_data3_i[15]}}, inport_data3_i};\n\nreg signed [31:0] i0;\nreg signed [31:0] mul0_a, mul0_b, mul1_a, mul1_b, mul2_a, mul2_b;\nreg signed [31:0] mul3_a, mul3_b, mul4_a, mul4_b;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst) begin\n    i0 <= 32'b0;\n    mul0_a <= 32'b0; mul0_b <= 32'b0;\n    mul1_a <= 32'b0; mul1_b <= 32'b0;\n    mul2_a <= 32'b0; mul2_b <= 32'b0;\n    mul3_a <= 32'b0; mul3_b <= 32'b0;\n    mul4_a <= 32'b0; mul4_b <= 32'b0;\nend else begin\n    case (inport_idx_i)\n    3'd0: begin\n        i0     <= block_in_0_1 + block_in_4_5;\n        mul0_a <= block_in_2_3; mul0_b <= C2_16;\n        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7; mul1_b <= C6_16;\n    end\n    3'd1: begin\n        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1; mul0_b <= C1_16;\n        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7; mul1_b <= C7_16;\n        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5; mul2_b <= C5_16;\n        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3; mul3_b <= C3_16;\n        mul4_a <= i0;            mul4_b <= C4_16;\n    end\n    3'd2: i0 <= block_in_0_1 - block_in_4_5;\n    3'd3: begin\n        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1; mul0_b <= C7_16;\n        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7; mul1_b <= C1_16;\n        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5; mul2_b <= C3_16;\n        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3; mul3_b <= C5_16;\n    end\n    3'd4: begin\n        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1; mul0_b <= C7_16;\n        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7; mul1_b <= C1_16;\n        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5; mul2_b <= C3_16;\n        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3; mul3_b <= C5_16;\n    end\n    3'd5: begin\n        mul0_a <= block_in_2_3; mul0_b <= C6_16;\n        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7; mul1_b <= C2_16;\n        mul4_a <= i0;            mul4_b <= C4_16;\n    end\n    default: ;\n    endcase\nend\n\nreg signed [31:0] mul0_q, mul1_q, mul2_q, mul3_q, mul4_q;\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst) begin\n    mul0_q <= 32'b0; mul1_q <= 32'b0; mul2_q <= 32'b0;\n    mul3_q <= 32'b0; mul4_q <= 32'b0;\nend else begin\n    mul0_q <= mul0_a * mul0_b;\n    mul1_q <= mul1_a * mul1_b;\n    mul2_q <= mul2_a * mul2_b;\n    mul3_q <= mul3_a * mul3_b;\n    mul4_q <= mul4_a * mul4_b;\nend\n\nreg signed [31:0] mul0, mul1, mul2, mul3, mul4;\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst) begin\n    mul0 <= 32'b0; mul1 <= 32'b0; mul2 <= 32'b0;\n    mul3 <= 32'b0; mul4 <= 32'b0;\nend else begin\n    mul0 <= mul0_q; mul1 <= mul1_q; mul2 <= mul2_q;\n    mul3 <= mul3_q; mul4 <= mul4_q;\nend\n\nreg        out_stg0_valid_q; reg [2:0] out_stg0_idx_q;\nreg        out_stg1_valid_q; reg [2:0] out_stg1_idx_q;\nreg        out_stg2_valid_q; reg [2:0] out_stg2_idx_q;\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst) begin\n    out_stg0_valid_q <= 1'b0; out_stg0_idx_q <= 3'b0;\n    out_stg1_valid_q <= 1'b0; out_stg1_idx_q <= 3'b0;\n    out_stg2_valid_q <= 1'b0; out_stg2_idx_q <= 3'b0;\nend else begin\n    out_stg0_valid_q <= inport_valid_i; out_stg0_idx_q <= inport_idx_i;\n    out_stg1_valid_q <= out_stg0_valid_q; out_stg1_idx_q <= out_stg0_idx_q;\n    out_stg2_valid_q <= out_stg1_valid_q; out_stg2_idx_q <= out_stg1_idx_q;\nend\n\nreg signed [31:0] o_s5, o_s6, o_s7;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t0, o_t1, o_t2, o_t3, o_t4, o_t5, o_t6, o_t7;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t6_5, o_t5_6;\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst) begin\n    o_s5 <= 32'b0; o_s6 <= 32'b0; o_s7 <= 32'b0;\n    o_t0 <= 32'b0; o_t1 <= 32'b0; o_t2 <= 32'b0; o_t3 <= 32'b0;\n    o_t4 <= 32'b0; o_t5 <= 32'b0; o_t6 <= 32'b0; o_t7 <= 32'b0;\n    o_t6_5 <= 32'b0; o_t5_6 <= 32'b0;\nend else begin\n    case (out_stg2_idx_q)\n    3'd0: o_t3 <= mul0 + mul1;\n    3'd1: begin\n        o_s7 <= mul0 + mul1; o_s6 <= mul2 + mul3; o_t0 <= mul4;\n    end\n    3'd2: begin\n        o_t0 <= o_t0 + o_t3; o_t3 <= o_t0 - o_t3; o_t7 <= o_s6 + o_s7;\n    end\n    3'd3: o_t4 <= (mul0 - mul1) + (mul2 - mul3);\n    3'd4: begin\n        o_t0 <= mul0 - mul1; o_s5 <= mul2 - mul3;\n    end\n    3'd5: begin\n        o_t3 <= mul0 - mul1; o_t4 <= mul4;\n        o_t5 <= o_t0 - o_s5; o_t6 <= o_s7 - o_s6;\n    end\n    3'd6: begin\n        o_t1 <= o_t4 + o_t3; o_t2 <= o_t4 - o_t3;\n        o_t6_5 <= o_t6 - o_t5; o_t5_6 <= o_t5 + o_t6;\n    end\n    default: begin\n        o_s5 <= (o_t6_5 * 181) / 256; o_s6 <= (o_t5_6 * 181) / 256;\n    end\n    endcase\nend\n\nreg        out_stg3_valid_q; reg [2:0] out_stg3_idx_q;\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst) begin\n    out_stg3_valid_q <= 1'b0; out_stg3_idx_q <= 3'b0;\nend else begin\n    out_stg3_valid_q <= out_stg2_valid_q; out_stg3_idx_q <= out_stg2_idx_q;\nend\n\nreg signed [31:0] block_out[0:7];\nreg signed [31:0] block_out_tmp;\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst) begin\n    block_out[0] <= 32'b0; block_out[1] <= 32'b0;\n    block_out[2] <= 32'b0; block_out[3] <= 32'b0;\n    block_out[4] <= 32'b0; block_out[5] <= 32'b0;\n    block_out[6] <= 32'b0; block_out[7] <= 32'b0;\n    block_out_tmp <= 32'b0;\nend else if (out_stg3_valid_q) begin\n    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd3) begin\n        block_out[0] <= ((o_t0 + o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out_tmp <= ((o_t0 - o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[3] <= ((o_t3 + o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[4] <= ((o_t3 - o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n    end\n    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd6) block_out[7] <= block_out_tmp;\n    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd7) begin\n        block_out[2] <= ((o_t2 + o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[5] <= ((o_t2 - o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[1] <= ((o_t1 + o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[6] <= ((o_t1 - o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n    end\nend\n\nreg [7:0] valid_q;\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst) valid_q <= 8'b0;\nelse if (img_start_i) valid_q <= 8'b0;\nelse valid_q <= {valid_q[6:0], out_stg3_valid_q};\n\nreg [5:0] ptr_q;\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst) ptr_q <= 6'd0;\nelse if (img_start_i) ptr_q <= 6'd0;\nelse if (outport_valid_o) ptr_q <= ptr_q + 6'd1;\n\nassign outport_valid_o = valid_q[6];\nassign outport_data_o = block_out[ptr_q[2:0]];\nassign outport_idx_o = ptr_q;\nendmodule\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// jpeg_idct_transpose: pre-compute combined read address\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule jpeg_idct_transpose(\n    input           clk\n    ,input           rst\n    ,input           img_start_i\n    ,input           img_end_i\n    ,input           inport_valid_i\n    ,input  [31:0]   inport_data_i\n    ,input  [5:0]    inport_idx_i\n    ,input           outport_ready_i\n    ,output          inport_accept_o\n    ,output          outport_valid_o\n    ,output [31:0]   outport_data0_o\n    ,output [31:0]   outport_data1_o\n    ,output [31:0]   outport_data2_o\n    ,output [31:0]   outport_data3_o\n    ,output [2:0]    outport_idx_o\n);\n\nreg         block_wr_q, block_rd_q;\nreg [5:0]   rd_idx_q;\nreg [3:0]   rd_addr_q;\n\nwire [4:0]  wr_ptr_w = {block_wr_q, inport_idx_i[3:0]};\n\nwire wr0_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd0);\nwire wr1_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd1);\nwire wr2_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd2);\nwire wr3_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd3);\n\n// Pre-compute combined read address = {block_rd_q, rd_addr_q}\nwire [4:0] rd_addr_w = {block_rd_q, rd_addr_q};\n\nreg [31:0] ram0 [0:31];\nreg [31:0] ram1 [0:31];\nreg [31:0] ram2 [0:31];\nreg [31:0] ram3 [0:31];\n\nreg [31:0] ram_read0_q, ram_read1_q, ram_read2_q, ram_read3_q;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk) begin\n    if (wr0_w) ram0[wr_ptr_w] <= inport_data_i;\n    ram_read0_q <= ram0[rd_addr_w];\nend\nalways @ (posedge clk) begin\n    if (wr1_w) ram1[wr_ptr_w] <= inport_data_i;\n    ram_read1_q <= ram1[rd_addr_w];\nend\nalways @ (posedge clk) begin\n    if (wr2_w) ram2[wr_ptr_w] <= inport_data_i;\n    ram_read2_q <= ram2[rd_addr_w];\nend\nalways @ (posedge clk) begin\n    if (wr3_w) ram3[wr_ptr_w] <= inport_data_i;\n    ram_read3_q <= ram3[rd_addr_w];\nend\n\nassign outport_data0_o = ram_read0_q;\nassign outport_data1_o = ram_read1_q;\nassign outport_data2_o = ram_read2_q;\nassign outport_data3_o = ram_read3_q;\n\nreg [1:0] block_ready_q;\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst) begin\n    block_ready_q <= 2'b0; block_wr_q <= 1'b0; block_rd_q <= 1'b0;\nend else if (img_start_i) begin\n    block_ready_q <= 2'b0; block_wr_q <= 1'b0; block_rd_q <= 1'b0;\nend else begin\n    if (inport_valid_i && inport_idx_i == 6'd63 && inport_accept_o) begin\n        block_ready_q[block_wr_q] <= 1'b1;\n        block_wr_q <= ~block_wr_q;\n    end\n    if (outport_valid_o && rd_idx_q == 6'd63) begin\n        block_ready_q[block_rd_q] <= 1'b0;\n        block_rd_q <= ~block_rd_q;\n    end\nend\n\nassign inport_accept_o = ~block_ready_q[block_wr_q];\n\nlocalparam STATE_IDLE = 2'd0, STATE_SETUP = 2'd1, STATE_ACTIVE = 2'd2;\nreg [1:0] state_q, next_state_r;\nalways @ * begin\n    next_state_r = state_q;\n    case (state_q)\n    STATE_IDLE:   if (block_ready_q[block_rd_q] && outport_ready_i) next_state_r = STATE_SETUP;\n    STATE_SETUP:  next_state_r = STATE_ACTIVE;\n    STATE_ACTIVE: if (outport_valid_o && rd_idx_q == 6'd63) next_state_r = STATE_IDLE;\n    default: ;\n    endcase\n    if (img_start_i) next_state_r = STATE_IDLE;\nend\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst) state_q <= STATE_IDLE;\nelse state_q <= next_state_r;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst) rd_idx_q <= 6'b0;\nelse if (img_start_i) rd_idx_q <= 6'b0;\nelse if (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE) rd_idx_q <= rd_idx_q + 6'd1;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst) rd_addr_q <= 4'b0;\nelse if (state_q == STATE_IDLE) rd_addr_q <= 4'b0;\nelse if (state_q == STATE_SETUP) rd_addr_q <= 4'd8;\nelse if (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE) begin\n    case (rd_idx_q[2:0])\n    3'd0: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q - 4'd8;\n    3'd1: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q + 4'd8;\n    3'd2: ;\n    3'd3: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q - 4'd8;\n    3'd4: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q + 4'd8;\n    3'd5: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q - 4'd8;\n    3'd6: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q + 4'd1;\n    3'd7: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q + 4'd8;\n    endcase\nend\n\nassign outport_valid_o = (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE);\nassign outport_idx_o = rd_idx_q[2:0];\nendmodule\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// jpeg_idct_y: column pass, OUT_SHIFT=15\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule jpeg_idct_y\n#(parameter OUT_SHIFT = 15, parameter INPUT_WIDTH = 32)\n(\n    input           clk\n    ,input           rst\n    ,input           img_start_i\n    ,input           img_end_i\n    ,input           inport_valid_i\n    ,input  [31:0]   inport_data0_i\n    ,input  [31:0]   inport_data1_i\n    ,input  [31:0]   inport_data2_i\n    ,input  [31:0]   inport_data3_i\n    ,input  [2:0]    inport_idx_i\n    ,output          outport_valid_o\n    ,output [31:0]   outport_data_o\n    ,output [5:0]    outport_idx_o\n);\n\nlocalparam [15:0] C1_16 = 4017, C2_16 = 3784, C3_16 = 3406, C4_16 = 2896;\nlocalparam [15:0] C5_16 = 2276, C6_16 = 1567, C7_16 = 799;\n\nwire signed [31:0] block_in_0_1 = inport_data0_i;\nwire signed [31:0] block_in_2_3 = inport_data1_i;\nwire signed [31:0] block_in_4_5 = inport_data2_i;\nwire signed [31:0] block_in_6_7 = inport_data3_i;\n\nreg signed [31:0] i0;\nreg signed [31:0] mul0_a, mul0_b, mul1_a, mul1_b, mul2_a, mul2_b;\nreg signed [31:0] mul3_a, mul3_b, mul4_a, mul4_b;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst) begin\n    i0 <= 32'b0;\n    mul0_a <= 32'b0; mul0_b <= 32'b0;\n    mul1_a <= 32'b0; mul1_b <= 32'b0;\n    mul2_a <= 32'b0; mul2_b <= 32'b0;\n    mul3_a <= 32'b0; mul3_b <= 32'b0;\n    mul4_a <= 32'b0; mul4_b <= 32'b0;\nend else begin\n    case (inport_idx_i)\n    3'd0: begin\n        i0 <= block_in_0_1 + block_in_4_5;\n        mul0_a <= block_in_2_3; mul0_b <= C2_16;\n        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7; mul1_b <= C6_16;\n    end\n    3'd1: begin\n        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1; mul0_b <= C1_16;\n        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7; mul1_b <= C7_16;\n        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5; mul2_b <= C5_16;\n        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3; mul3_b <= C3_16;\n        mul4_a <= i0;            mul4_b <= C4_16;\n    end\n    3'd2: i0 <= block_in_0_1 - block_in_4_5;\n    3'd3: begin\n        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1; mul0_b <= C7_16;\n        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7; mul1_b <= C1_16;\n        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5; mul2_b <= C3_16;\n        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3; mul3_b <= C5_16;\n    end\n    3'd4: begin\n        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1; mul0_b <= C7_16;\n        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7; mul1_b <= C1_16;\n        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5; mul2_b <= C3_16;\n        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3; mul3_b <= C5_16;\n    end\n    3'd5: begin\n        mul0_a mul0_q, mul1_q, mul2_q, mul3_q, mul4_q;\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst) begin\n    mul0_q <= 32'b0; mul1_q <= 32'b0; mul2_q <= 32'b0;\n    mul3_q <= 32'b0; mul4_q <= 32'b0;\nend else begin\n    mul0_q <= mul0_a * mul0_b;\n    mul1_q <= mul1_a * mul1_b;\n    mul2_q <= mul2_a * mul2_b;\n    mul3_q <= mul3_a * mul3_b;\n    mul4_q <= mul4_a * mul4_b;\nend\n\nreg signed [31:0] mul0, mul1, mul2, mul3, mul4;\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst) begin\n    mul0 <= 32'b0; mul1 <= 32'b0; mul2 <= 32'b0;\n    mul3 <= 32'b0; mul4 <= 32'b0;\nend else begin\n    mul0 <= mul0_q; mul1 <= mul1_q; mul2 <= mul2_q;\n    mul3 <= mul3_q; mul4 <= mul4_q;\nend\n\nreg        out_stg0_valid_q; reg [2:0] out_stg0_idx_q;\nreg        out_stg1_valid_q; reg [2:0] out_stg1_idx_q;\nreg        out_stg2_valid_q; reg [2:0] out_stg2_idx_q;\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst) begin\n    out_stg0_valid_q <= 1'b0; out_stg0_idx_q <= 3'b0;\n    out_stg1_valid_q <= 1'b0; out_stg1_idx_q <= 3'b0;\n    out_stg2_valid_q <= out_stg2_valid_q <= out_stg1_valid_q; out_stg2_idx_q <= out_stg1_idx_q;\nend\n\nreg signed [31:0] o_s5, o_s6, o_s7;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t0, o_t1, o_t2, o_t3, o_t4, o_t5, o_t6, o_t7;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t6_5, o_t5_6;\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst) begin\n    o_s5 <= 32'b0; o_s6 <= 32'b0; o_s7 <= 32'b0;\n    o_t0 <= 32'b0; o_t1 <= 32'b0; o_t2 <= 32'b0; o_t3 <= 32'b0;\n    o_t4 <= 32'b0; o_t5 <= 32'b0; o_t6 <= 32'b0; o_t7 <= 32'b0;\n    o_t6_5 <= 32'b0; o_t5_6 <= 32'b0;\nend else begin\n    case (out_stg2_idx_q)\n    3'd0: o_t3 <= mul0 + mul1;\n    3'd1: begin\n        o_s7 <= mul0 + 3'd6: begin\n        o_t1 <= o_t4 + o_t3; o_t2 <= o_t4 - o_t3;\n        o_t6_5 <= o_t6 - o_t5; o_t5_6 <= o_t5 + o_t6;\n    end\n    default: begin\n        o_s5 <= (o_t6_5 * 181) / 256; o_s6 <= [ASSISTANT]
Good improvement! 8.95M (from 9.25M). The critical path is still the transpose RAM 32:1 mux (rd_addr_q → ram_read_q). The key bottleneck is the 5-bit address decode through the 32-entry mux. 

Let me try a more radical approach: eliminate the transpose entirely by merging it into the pipeline. Instead of a transpose buffer, I can directly write the x-pass output to a 2D array indexed by row and column, then read it out in column order for the y-pass. But actually, the transpose is already doing exactly that with 4 RAMs. The issue is the 32:1 mux depth.

Let me try a different approach: use a 64-entry flat array instead of 4x32-entry RAMs, which changes the mux from 32:1 to 64:1 but allows simpler addressing. Actually that's worse. 

Let me instead try to register the rd_addr_q earlier or pre-compute the address. The problem is rd_addr_q is a 4-bit counter that goes through a case statement. Let me try to pre-compute next address:

[ASSISTANT]
Now, let me try a different approach. Instead of modifying the transpose, let me focus on the x and y IDCT modules themselves. The `mul_q → mul` pipeline stage (two sequential registers for the same data) wastes area. I need to keep the pipeline depth but I can merge them: instead of `mul_q` (registered product) → `mul` (registered copy), I can compute the product directly and register it once, then adjust the pipeline depth of the valid/idx tracking.

Actually wait - in v3 the mul_q→mul pipeline IS needed because the output processing case statement uses `mul0` through `mul4` at `out_stg2_idx` which is 3 cycles after input. The mul_q is at cycle 1, mul is at cycle 2, and the output case at cycle 3 uses mul. But in the original, the schedule is:
- Cycle 0: input, mul_a/mul_b loaded
- Cycle 1: mul_q = product
- Cycle 2: mul = mul_q (copy)
- Cycle 3: out_stg2 uses mul

If I remove the mul=mul_q stage, mul_q would be at cycle 1, and I need out_stg2 to be at cycle 2 instead of 3. That means I need to remove one pipeline stage from the valid/idx tracking.

Let me try this in the x and y modules. Let me create v5 based on v3 but with the mul pipeline removed:

[ASSISTANT]
Same issue as v2 - the valid_q needs to be 8 bits not 7. The problem is that when we remove the mul pipeline stage, the output timing shifts by 1 cycle, which breaks the overlap between the x-pass output and the transpose input. The transpose expects data at specific cycle offsets. Let me think more carefully...

The issue is that the x-pass output valid signal is 1 cycle earlier, but the transpose and y-pass still expect the original timing. The x-pass valid_q[6] is the output valid. If we reduce the pipeline by 1, valid_q[5] fires 1 cycle earlier, but the transpose doesn't know about this. Actually the transpose just accepts whatever comes in, so the issue is likely in the y-pass: its output is 1 cycle earlier, so `ptr_q` and `valid_q` don't align properly with the expected output positions.

Let me go back to v3 (which works at 8.95M) and try a different optimization approach. Instead of removing pipeline stages, let me try to reduce the transpose RAM mux delay by using a different memory organization.

The delay is always 2100 regardless of target. The critical path starts at `rd_addr_q` (DFF Q→QN delay 1403ps!) then goes through the 32:1 mux decode (~700ps). The huge DFF delay is because the QN (inverted) output is being used.

Let me try a radically different approach: use an 8x8 flat register array with explicit row/column decode instead of 4 32-entry RAMs. The transpose stores 64 values per block (32-bit each), double-buffered = 128 values. Currently it uses 4×32=128 entries split across 4 RAMs.

inport_data2_i};\nwire signed [31:0] block_in_6_7 = {{16{inport_data3_i[15]}}, inport_data3_i};\n\nreg signed [31:0] i0;\nreg signed [31:0] mul0_a, mul0_b, mul1_a, mul1_b, mul2_a, mul2_b;\nreg signed [31:0] mul3_a, mul3_b, mul4_a, mul4_b;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst) begin\n    i0 <= 32'b0;\n    mul0_a <= 32'b0; mul0_b <= 32'b0;\n    mul1_a <= 32'b0; mul1_b <= 32'b0;\n    mul2_a <= 32'b0; mul2_b <= 32'b0;\n    mul3_a <= 32'b0; mul3_b <= 32'b0;\n    mul4_a <= 32'b0; mul4_b <= 32'b0;\nend else begin\n    case (inport_idx_i)\n    3'd0: begin\n        i0     <= block_in_0_1 + block_in_4_5;\n        mul0_a <= block_in_2_3; mul0_b <= C2_16;\n        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7; mul1_b <= C6_16;\n    end\n    3'd1: begin\n        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1; mul0_b <= C1_16;\n        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7; mul1_b <= C7_16;\n        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5; mul2_b <= C5_16;\n        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3; mul3_b <= C3_16;\n        mul4_a <= i0;            mul4_b <= C4_16;\n    end\n    3'd2: i0 <= block_in_0_1 - block_in_4_5;\n    3'd3: begin\n        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1; mul0_b <= C7_16;\n        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7; mul1_b <= C1_16;\n        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5; mul2_b <= C3_16;\n        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3; mul3_b <= C5_16;\n    end\n    3'd4: begin\n        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1; mul0_b <= C7_16;\n        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7; mul1_b <= C1_16;\n        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5; mul2_b <= C3_16;\n        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3; mul3_b <= C5_16;\n    end\n    3'd5: begin\n        mul0_a <= block_in_2_3; mul0_b <= C6_16;\n        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7; mul1_b <= C2_16;\n        mul4_a <= i0;            mul4_b <= C4_16;\n    end\n    default: ;\n    endcase\nend\n\nreg signed [31:0] mul0_q, mul1_q, mul2_q, mul3_q, mul4_q;\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst) begin\n    mul0_q <= 32'b0; mul1_q <= 32'b0; mul2_q <= 32'b0;\n    mul3_q <= 32'b0; mul4_q <= 32'b0;\nend else begin\n    mul0_q <= mul0_a * mul0_b;\n    mul1_q <= mul1_a * mul1_b;\n    mul2_q <= mul2_a * mul2_b;\n    mul3_q <= mul3_a * mul3_b;\n    mul4_q <= mul4_a * mul4_b;\nend\n\nreg signed [31:0] mul0, mul1, mul2, mul3, mul4;\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst) begin\n    mul0 <= 32'b0; mul1 <= 32'b0; mul2 <= 32'b0;\n    mul3 <= 32'b0; mul4 <= 32'b0;\nend else begin\n    mul0 <= mul0_q; mul1 <= mul1_q; mul2 <= mul2_q;\n    mul3 <= mul3_q; mul4 <= mul4_q;\nend\n\nreg        out_stg0_valid_q; reg [2:0] out_stg0_idx_q;\nreg        out_stg1_valid_q; reg [2:0] out_stg1_idx_q;\nreg        out_stg2_valid_q; reg [2:0] out_stg2_idx_q;\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst) begin\n    out_stg0_valid_q <= 1'b0; out_stg0_idx_q <= 3'b0;\n    out_stg1_valid_q <= 1'b0; out_stg1_idx_q <= 3'b0;\n    out_stg2_valid_q <= 1'b0; out_stg2_idx_q <= 3'b0;\nend else begin\n    out_stg0_valid_q <= inport_valid_i; out_stg0_idx_q <= inport_idx_i;\n    out_stg1_valid_q <= out_stg0_valid_q; out_stg1_idx_q <= out_stg0_idx_q;\n    out_stg2_valid_q <= out_stg1_valid_q; out_stg2_idx_q <= out_stg1_idx_q;\nend\n\nreg signed [31:0] o_s5, o_s6, o_s7;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t0, o_t1, o_t2, o_t3, o_t4, o_t5, o_t6, o_t7;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t6_5, o_t5_6;\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst) begin\n    o_s5 <= 32'b0; o_s6 <= 32'b0; o_s7 <= 32'b0;\n    o_t0 <= 32'b0; o_t1 <= 32'b0; o_t2 <= 32'b0; o_t3 <= 32'b0;\n    o_t4 <= 32'b0; o_t5 <= 32'b0; o_t6 <= 32'b0; o_t7 <= 32'b0;\n    o_t6_5 <= 32'b0; o_t5_6 <= 32'b0;\nend else begin\n    case (out_stg2_idx_q)\n    3'd0: o_t3 <= mul0 + mul1;\n    3'd1: begin\n        o_s7 <= mul0 + mul1; o_s6 <= mul2 + mul3; o_t0 <= mul4;\n    end\n    3'd2: begin\n        o_t0 <= o_t0 + o_t3; o_t3 <= o_t0 - o_t3; o_t7 <= o_s6 + o_s7;\n    end\n    3'd3: o_t4 <= (mul0 - mul1) + (mul2 - mul3);\n    3'd4: begin\n        o_t0 <= mul0 - mul1; o_s5 <= mul2 - mul3;\n    end\n    3'd5: begin\n        o_t3 <= mul0 - mul1; o_t4 <= mul4;\n        o_t5 <= o_t0 - o_s5; o_t6 <= o_s7 - o_s6;\n    end\n    3'd6: begin\n        o_t1 <= o_t4 + o_t3; o_t2 <= o_t4 - o_t3;\n        o_t6_5 <= o_t6 - o_t5; o_t5_6 <= o_t5 + o_t6;\n    end\n    default: begin\n        o_s5 <= (o_t6_5 * 181) / 256; o_s6 <= (o_t5_6 * 181) / 256;\n    end\n    endcase\nend\n\nreg        out_stg3_valid_q; reg [2:0] out_stg3_idx_q;\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst) begin\n    out_stg3_valid_q <= 1'b0; out_stg3_idx_q <= 3'b0;\nend else begin\n    out_stg3_valid_q <= out_stg2_valid_q; out_stg3_idx_q <= out_stg2_idx_q;\nend\n\nreg signed [31:0] block_out[0:7];\nreg signed [31:0] block_out_tmp;\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst) begin\n    block_out[0] <= 32'b0; block_out[1] <= 32'b0;\n    block_out[2] <= 32'b0; block_out[3] <= 32'b0;\n    block_out[4] <= 32'b0; block_out[5] <= 32'b0;\n    block_out[6] <= 32'b0; block_out[7] <= 32'b0;\n    block_out_tmp <= 32'b0;\nend else if (out_stg3_valid_q) begin\n    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd3) begin\n        block_out[0] <= ((o_t0 + o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out_tmp <= ((o_t0 - o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[3] <= ((o_t3 + o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[4] <= ((o_t3 - o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n    end\n    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd6) block_out[7] <= block_out_tmp;\n    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd7) begin\n        block_out[2] <= ((o_t2 + o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[5] <= ((o_t2 - o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[1] <= ((o_t1 + o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[6] <= ((o_t1 - o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n    end\nend\n\nreg [7:0] valid_q;\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst) valid_q <= 8'b0;\nelse if (img_start_i) valid_q <= 8'b0;\nelse valid_q <= {valid_q[6:0], out_stg3_valid_q};\n\nreg [5:0] ptr_q;\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst) ptr_q <= 6'd0;\nelse if (img_start_i) ptr_q <= 6'd0;\nelse if (outport_valid_o) ptr_q <= ptr_q + 6'd1;\n\nassign outport_valid_o = valid_q[6];\nassign outport_data_o = block_out[ptr_q[2:0]];\nassign outport_idx_o = ptr_q;\nendmodule\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// jpeg_idct_transpose: 8-row bank structure with 8:1 mux\n// Each bank stores 8 values × 32 bits = 256 bits per row.\n// Double-buffered: 2 banks × 8 rows × 4 lanes.\n// Write: sequential by idx (0..63), write address = {wr_block, idx[3:0]},\n//   bank select = idx[5:4].\n// Read: row-major by rd_row (0..7), all 4 lanes read simultaneously.\n//   The read sequence visits rows in the transpose order.\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule jpeg_idct_transpose(\n    input           clk\n    ,input           rst\n    ,input           img_start_i\n    ,input           img_end_i\n    ,input           inport_valid_i\n    ,input  [31:0]   inport_data_i\n    ,input  [5:0]    inport_idx_i\n    ,input           outport_ready_i\n    ,output          inport_accept_o\n    ,output          outport_valid_o\n    ,output [31:0]   outport_data0_o\n    ,output [31:0]   outport_data1_o\n    ,output [31:0]   outport_data2_o\n    ,output [31:0]   outport_data3_o\n    ,output [2:0]    outport_idx_o\n);\n\n// Write side\nreg         block_wr_q, block_rd_q;\nwire        wr_en = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o;\nwire [1:0]  wr_bank = inport_idx_i[5:4];\nwire [3:0]  wr_col = inport_idx_i[3:0];\n\n// Read side\nreg [5:0]   rd_idx_q;\nreg [2:0]   rd_row_q;  // 3-bit row select for 8:1 mux\n\n// Double-buffered storage: 2 blocks × 8 rows × 4 lanes × 32 bits\n// Organized as [block][row][lane] = 32-bit\n// bank0: rows 0-7 for block 0, bank1: rows 0-7 for block 1\n// Each bank has 4 lanes (one per data output)\nreg [31:0] bank0_lane0 [0:7];\nreg [31:0] bank0_lane1 [0:7];\nreg [31:0] bank0_lane2 [0:7];\nreg [31:0] bank0_lane3 [0:7];\nreg [31:0] bank1_lane0 [0:7];\nreg [31:0] bank1_lane1 [0:7];\nreg [31:0] bank1_lane2 [0:7];\nreg [31:0] bank1_lane3 [0:7];\n\n// The write mapping: inport_idx_i is the x-pass output index.\n// idx[5:0] = {row[2:0], col[2:0]} where row = idx[5:3], col = idx[2:0]\n// But the x-pass outputs in row-major: idx 0-7 = row 0, 8-15 = row 1, etc.\n// The 4 data lanes come from the 4 x-pass output values per beat.\n// Actually the x-pass outputs 8 values per row, one per beat (idx 0-7).\n// Wait no - the x-pass outputs one 32-bit value per beat, idx 0-7 per row.\n// The transpose writes: addr = {block, idx[3:0]}, bank = idx[5:4]\n// So for a full block (64 beats), idx 0-7 go to bank0 col 0-7,\n// idx 8-15 go to bank1 col 0-7, etc.\n// Read order: the transpose reads in column-major order.\n// rd_addr_q sequence: 0,8,16,24,1,9,17,25,... (column-major of the 8x4 grid)\n\n// Actually let me just replicate the original structure but with 8-entry arrays\n// The original uses 32-entry RAMs with 5-bit address {block, addr[3:0]}.\n// The read address rd_addr_q is 4 bits, and the combined is {block_rd_q, rd_addr_q}.\n// rd_addr_q counts 0,8,16,24,1,9,...,7,15,23,31 (column-major of 4×8 grid)\n\n// The mapping: address[4:0] = {block, [3:0]}\n// [3:0] encodes {row[2:0], col[1:0]} or something like that.\n// Let me just keep the same address but use 4 banks of 8-entry arrays.\n// addr[3:0]: bits [3] = row_hi, [2:1] = ?, [0] = ?\n\n// Actually, the simplest approach: keep 32-entry RAMs but split into\n// 8-entry sub-arrays with 4:1 mux + registered sub-array select.\n\n// Let me try a different approach: use the 8:1 structure directly.\n// For each of 4 lanes, store 2×8 = 16 entries (2 blocks × 8 rows).\n// Write: bank[wr_bank][wr_col] <= data  (wr_bank=idx[5:4], wr_col=idx[3:0])\n// But wait, that's 4 banks × 16 entries = 64 entries per lane. Same as 32-entry RAM.\n\n// Let me just try: for each lane, use a 2D array [1:0][7:0] indexed by [block][row]\n// where row = idx[5:3] (which row of the 8x8 block) and col = idx[2:0] (which column)\n// But the original transpose uses idx[5:4] for bank and idx[3:0] for address.\n// The write mapping: addr = {block, idx[3:0]}, so 32 entries per RAM.\n// idx[3:0] goes 0-7 (row 0), 8-15 (row 1), ..., but idx[5:4] = 0,1,2,3 for the 4 banks.\n// So idx 0-7: bank0, addr 0-7\n// idx 8-15: bank1, addr 0-7\n// idx 16-23: bank2, addr 0-7\n// idx 24-31: bank3, addr 0-7\n// idx 32-39: bank0, addr 8-15\n// idx 40-47: bank1, addr 8-15\n// etc.\n\n// The read address rd_addr_q goes: 0,8,16,24,1,9,17,25,...\n// This means for each read beat, we read from all 4 banks at the same address.\n// The address encodes: addr[3] selects which half (rows 0-3 vs 4-7),\n// addr[2:0] selects the column within the half.\n// But we read from all 4 banks simultaneously.\n\n// So if I restructure as 4 banks × 8 entries (addr[2:0]) + 2 halves (addr[3]),\n// I get 4 banks × 2 halves × 8 entries.\n// The read uses a 4:1 mux on the 2-bit half select + 8:1 mux on the 3-bit addr.\n\n// Let me just use 4 banks of 2×8 arrays:\n// bankX[2][8], read as bankX[rd_addr_q[3]][rd_addr_q[2:0]]\n// Write: bankX[wr_bank][wr_col[2:0]] where wr_col = idx[3:0] but we need [3] for half\n\n// Actually this is getting complicated. Let me just try the straightforward approach:\n// Replace each 32-entry RAM with explicit case-based 32:1 mux but split into\n// two levels: first select one of 4 groups of 8, then 8:1 within the group.\n// This should give Yosys a better structure to work with.\n\n// Actually, let me try something simpler: just make rd_addr_q a registered\n// output by computing the NEXT address combinationally and registering it.\n// This way the 32:1 mux input is stable and registered.\n\n// Compute next rd_addr combinationally, register it\nwire [3:0] rd_addr_next;\nreg  [3:0] rd_addr_d;\n\nalways @(*) begin\n    case (state_q)\n    STATE_IDLE:   rd_addr_d = 4'd0;\n    STATE_SETUP:  rd_addr_d = 4'd8;\n    STATE_ACTIVE: begin\n        case (rd_idx_q[2:0])\n        3'd0: rd_addr_d = rd_addr_q - 4'd8;\n        3'd1: rd_addr_d = rd_addr_q + 4'd8;\n        3'd2: rd_addr_d = rd_addr_q;\n        3'd3: rd_addr_d = rd_addr_q - 4'd8;\n        3'd4: rd_addr_d = rd_addr_q + 4'd8;\n        3'd5: rd_addr_d = rd_addr_q - 4'd8;\n        3'd6: rd_addr_d = rd_addr_q + 4'd1;\n        3'd7: rd_addr_d = rd_addr_q + 4'd8;\n        endcase\n    end\n    default: rd_addr_d = 4'd0;\n    endcase\n    if (img_start_i) rd_addr_d = 4'd0;\nend\n\nassign rd_addr_next = rd_addr_d;\n\nwire [4:0]  wr_ptr_w = {block_wr_q, inport_idx_i[3:0]};\n\nwire wr0_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd0);\nwire wr1_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd1);\nwire wr2_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd2);\nwire wr3_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd3);\n\nwire [4:0] rd_addr_w = {block_rd_q, rd_addr_q};\n\nreg [31:0] ram0 [0:31];\nreg [31:0] ram1 [0:31];\nreg [31:0] ram2 [0:31];\nreg [31:0] ram3 [0:31];\n\nreg [31:0] ram_read0_q, ram_read1_q, ram_read2_q, ram_read3_q;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk) begin\n    if (wr0_w) ram0[wr_ptr_w] <= inport_data_i;\n    ram_read0_q <= ram0[rd_addr_w];\nend\nalways @ (posedge clk) begin\n    if (wr1_w) ram1[wr_ptr_w] <= inport_data_i;\n    ram_read1_q <= ram1[rd_addr_w];\nend\nalways @ (posedge clk) begin\n    if (wr2_w) ram2[wr_ptr_w] <= inport_data_i;\n    ram_read2_q <= ram2[rd_addr_w];\nend\nalways @ (posedge clk) begin\n    if (wr3_w) ram3[wr_ptr_w] <= inport_data_i;\n    ram_read3_q <= ram3[rd_addr_w];\nend\n\nassign outport_data0_o = ram_read0_q;\nassign outport_data1_o = ram_read1_q;\nassign outport_data2_o = ram_read2_q;\nassign outport_data3_o = ram_read3_q;\n\nreg [1:0] block_ready_q;\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst) begin\n    block_ready_q <= 2'b0; block_wr_q <= 1'b0; block_rd_q <= 1'b0;\nend else if (img_start_i) begin\n    block_ready_q <= 2'b0; block_wr_q <= 1'b0; block_rd_q <= 1'b0;\nend else begin\n    if (inport_valid_i && inport_idx_i == 6'd63 && inport_accept_o) begin\n        block_ready_q[block_wr_q] <= 1'b1;\n        block_wr_q <= ~block_wr_q;\n    end\n    if (outport_valid_o && rd_idx_q == 6'd63) begin\n        block_ready_q[block_rd_q] <= 1'b0;\n        block_rd_q <= ~block_rd_q;\n    end\nend\n\nassign inport_accept_o = ~block_ready_q[block_wr_q];\n\nlocalparam STATE_IDLE = 2'd0, STATE_SETUP = 2'd1, STATE_ACTIVE = 2'd2;\nreg [1:0] state_q, next_state_r;\nalways @ * begin\n    next_state_r = state_q;\n    case (state_q)\n    STATE_IDLE:   if (block_ready_q[block_rd_q] && outport_ready_i) next_state_r = STATE_SETUP;\n    STATE_SETUP:  next_state_r = STATE_ACTIVE;\n    STATE_ACTIVE: if (outport_valid_o && rd_idx_q == 6'd63) next_state_r = STATE_IDLE;\n    default: ;\n    endcase\n    if (img_start_i) next_state_r = STATE_IDLE;\nend\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst) state_q <= STATE_IDLE;\nelse state_q <= next_state_r;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst) rd_idx_q <= 6'b0;\nelse if (img_start_i) rd_idx_q <= 6'b0;\nelse if (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE) rd_idx_q <= rd_idx_q + 6'd1;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst) rd_addr_q <= 4'b0;\nelse rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_next;\n\nassign outport_valid_o = (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE);\nassign outport_idx_o = rd_idx_q[2:0];\nendmodule\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// jpeg_idct_y: column pass, OUT_SHIFT=15 (same as v3)\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule jpeg_idct_y\n#(parameter OUT_SHIFT = 15, parameter INPUT_WIDTH = 32)\n(\n    input           clk\n    ,input           rst\n    ,input           img_start_i\n    ,input           img_end_i\n    ,input           inport_valid_i\n    ,input  [31:0]   inport_data0_i\n    ,input  [31:0]   inport_data1_i\n    ,input  [31:0]   inport_data2_i\n    ,input  [31:0]   inport_data3_i\n    ,input  [2:0]    inport_idx_i\n    ,output          outport_valid_o\n    ,output [31:0]   outport_data_o\n    ,output [5:0]    outport_idx_o\n);\n\nlocalparam [15:0] C1_16 = 4017, C2_16 = 3784, C3_16 = 3406, C4_16 = 2896;\nlocalparam [15:0] C5_16 = 2276, C6_16 = 1567, C7_16 = 799;\n\nwire signed [31:0] block_in_0_1 = inport_data0_i;\nwire signed [31:0] block_in_2_3 = inport_data1_i;\nwire signed [31:0] block_in_4_5 = inport_data2_i;\nwire signed [31:0] block_in_6_7 = inport_data3_i;\n\nreg signed [31:0] i0;\nreg signed [31:0] mul0_a, mul0_b, mul1_a, mul1_b, mul2_a, mul2_b;\nreg signed [31:0] mul3_a, mul3_b, mul4_a, mul4_b;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst) begin\n    i0 <= 32'b0;\n    mul0_a <= 32'b0; mul0_b <= 32'b0;\n    mul1_a <= 32'b0; mul1_b <= 32'b0;\n    mul2_a <= 32'b0; mul2_b <= 32'b0;\n    mul3_a <= 32'b0; mul3_b <= 32'b0;\n    mul4_a <= 32'b0; mul4_b <= 32'b0;\nend else begin\n    case (inport_idx_i)\n    3'd0: begin\n        i0 <= block_in_0_1 + block_in_4_5;\n        mul0_a <= block_in_2_3; mul0_b <= C2_16;\n        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7; mul1_b <= C6_16;\n    end\n    3'd1: begin\n        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1; mul0_b <= C1_16;\n        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7; mul1_b <= C7_16;\n        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5; mul2_b <= C5_16;\n        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3; mul3_b <= C3_16;\n        mul4_a <= i0;            mul4_b <= C4_16;\n    end\n    3'd2: i0 <= block_in_0_1 - block_in_4_5;\n    3'd3: begin\n        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1; mul0_b <= C7_16;\n        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7; mul1_b <= C1_16;\n        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5; mul2_b <= C3_16;\n        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3; mul3_b <= C5_16;\n    end\n    3'd4: begin\n        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1; mul0_b <= [ASSISTANT]
The address pipeline register delays data by 1 cycle, but the rd_idx_q still counts at the same rate, so data and idx are misaligned. I need to also delay the idx and valid signals. Let me fix this - delay `outport_idx_o` and `outport_valid_o` to align with the delayed data.

The issue is that `rd_idx_q` increments during ACTIVE, but the data arrives 1 cycle later. So the data for rd_idx_q=k arrives when rd_idx_q=k+1. I need to delay the idx output by 1 cycle too:

mul3_b <= C5_16; end\n    3'd5: begin mul0_a <= block_in_2_3; mul0_b <= C6_16; mul1_a <= block_in_6_7; mul1_b <= C2_16; mul4_a <= i0; mul4_b <= C4_16; end\n    default: ;\n    endcase\nend\nreg signed [31:0] mul0_q, mul1_q, mul2_q, mul3_q, mul4_q;\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst) begin mul0_q <= 0; mul1_q <= 0; mul2_q <= 0; mul3_q <= 0; mul4_q <= 0; end\nelse begin mul0_q <= mul0_a * mul0_b; mul1_q <= mul1_a * mul1_b; mul2_q <= mul2_a * mul2_b; mul3_q <= mul3_a * mul3_b; mul4_q <= mul4_a * mul4_b; end\nreg signed [31:0] mul0, mul1, mul2, mul3, mul4;\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst) begin o_t2, o_t3, o_t4, o_t5, o_t6, o_t7;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t6_5, o_t5_6;\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst) begin o_s5 <= 0; o_s6 <= 0; o_s7 <= 0; o_t0 <= 0; o_t1 <= 0; o_t2 <= 0; o_t3 <= 0; o_t4 <= 0; o_t5 <= 0; o_t6 <= 0; o_t7 <= 0; o_t6_5 <= 0; o_t5_6 <= 0; end\nelse begin\n    case (out_stg2_idx_q)\n    3'd0: o_t3 <= mul0 + mul1;\n    3'd1: begin o_s7 <= mul0 + mul1; o_s6 <= mul2 + mul3; o_t0 <= o_t5 + o_t6; end\n    default: begin o_s5 <= (o_t6_5 * 181) / 256; o_s6 <= (o_t5_6 * 181) / 256; end\n    endcase\nend\nreg out_stg3_valid_q; reg [2:0] out_stg3_idx_q;\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst) begin out_stg3_valid_q <= 0; out_stg3_idx_q <= 0; end\nelse begin out_stg3_valid_q <= out_stg2_valid_q; out_stg3_idx_q <= out_stg2_idx_q; end\nreg signed [31:0] block_out[0:7];\nreg signed [31:0] block_out_tmp;\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst) begin block_out[0] <= 0; block_out[1] <= 0; block_out[2] <= 0; block_out[3] <= 0; block_out[4] <= 0; block_out[5] <= 0; block_out[6] <= 0; block_out[7] <= 0; block_out_tmp <= 0; end\nelse if (out_stg3_valid_q) begin\n    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd3) begin block_out[0] <= ((o_t0 + o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT); block_out_tmp <= ((o_t0 - o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT); block_out[3] <= ((o_t3 + o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT); block_out[4] <= ((o_t3 - o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT); end\n    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd6) block_out[7] <= block_out_tmp;\n    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd7) begin block_out[2] <= ((o_t2 + o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT); block_out[5] <= ((o_t2 - o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT); block_out[1] <= ((o_t1 + o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT); block_out[6] <= ((o_t1 - o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
